829					preGenDungeonEntranceSettings.BuriedEntranceYOffset = 20 + num;

831					preGenDungeonEntranceSettings.RoughHeight = 120;
832					break;
833				}
834				preGenDungeonEntranceSettings.RandomSeed = genRand.Next();
835				return preGenDungeonEntranceSettings;

25		public void LegacyDungeonLayout(DungeonData data, GenerationProgress progress, UnifiedRandom genRand, ushort tileType, ushort crackedTileType, ushort wallType, int steps, int maxSteps, ref int roomDelay)
26		{
27			if (data.genVars.preGenDungeonEntranceSettings.PrecalculateEntrancePosition)
28			{
29				data.genVars.generatingDungeonPositionX = -10 + (int)data.genVars.dungeonEntrancePosition.X + genRand.Next(20);
30				data.genVars.generatingDungeonPositionY = (int)data.genVars.dungeonEntrancePosition.Y + 30;
31			}
32			data.outerProgressionBounds = new DungeonBounds[1];
33			data.outerProgressionBounds[0] = data.genVars.outerPotentialDungeonBounds;
34			LegacyDungeonHallSettings legacyDungeonHallSettings = new LegacyDungeonHallSettings
35			{
36				StyleData = data.genVars.dungeonStyle,
37				RandomSeed = genRand.Next()
38			};
39			LegacyDungeonRoomSettings legacyDungeonRoomSettings = new LegacyDungeonRoomSettings
40			{
41				StyleData = data.genVars.dungeonStyle,
42				RandomSeed = genRand.Next()
43			};
44			DungeonCrawler.MakeDungeon_GetRoom(new LegacyDungeonRoomSettings
45			{
46				StyleData = data.genVars.dungeonStyle,
47				StartingRoom = true,
48				RandomSeed = genRand.Next(),
49				RoomPosition = new Point(data.genVars.generatingDungeonPositionX, data.genVars.generatingDungeonPositionY)
50			}).GenerateRoom(data);
51			while (steps > 0)
52			{
53				data.dungeonBounds.UpdateBounds(data.genVars.generatingDungeonPositionX, data.genVars.generatingDungeonPositionY);
54				steps--;
55				int num = (maxSteps - steps) / maxSteps * 60;
56				DungeonUtils.UpdateDungeonProgress(progress, (float)num / 100f, Language.GetTextValue("WorldGeneration.DungeonRoomsAndHalls"));
57				if (roomDelay > 0)
58				{
59					roomDelay--;
60				}
61				if ((roomDelay == 0) & (genRand.Next(3) == 0))
62				{
63					roomDelay = 5;
64					if (genRand.Next(2) == 0)
65					{
66						int generatingDungeonPositionX = data.genVars.generatingDungeonPositionX;
67						int generatingDungeonPositionY = data.genVars.generatingDungeonPositionY;
68						legacyDungeonHallSettings.RandomSeed = genRand.Next();
69						DungeonCrawler.MakeDungeon_GetHall_Legacy(legacyDungeonHallSettings).GenerateHall(data, data.genVars.generatingDungeonPositionX, data.genVars.generatingDungeonPositionY);
70						if (genRand.Next(2) == 0)
71						{
72							legacyDungeonHallSettings.RandomSeed = genRand.Next();
73							DungeonCrawler.MakeDungeon_GetHall_Legacy(legacyDungeonHallSettings).GenerateHall(data, data.genVars.generatingDungeonPositionX, data.genVars.generatingDungeonPositionY);
74						}
75						legacyDungeonRoomSettings.RandomSeed = genRand.Next();
76						legacyDungeonRoomSettings.RoomPosition = new Point(data.genVars.generatingDungeonPositionX, data.genVars.generatingDungeonPositionY);
77						DungeonCrawler.MakeDungeon_GetRoom(legacyDungeonRoomSettings).GenerateRoom(data);
78						data.genVars.generatingDungeonPositionX = generatingDungeonPositionX;
79						data.genVars.generatingDungeonPositionY = generatingDungeonPositionY;
80					}
81					else

84						legacyDungeonRoomSettings.RoomPosition = new Point(data.genVars.generatingDungeonPositionX, data.genVars.generatingDungeonPositionY);
85						DungeonCrawler.MakeDungeon_GetRoom(legacyDungeonRoomSettings).GenerateRoom(data);
86					}

91					DungeonCrawler.MakeDungeon_GetHall_Legacy(legacyDungeonHallSettings).GenerateHall(data, data.genVars.generatingDungeonPositionX, data.genVars.generatingDungeonPositionY);

101						center = GetRoomCenterForDungeonFeature(data, dungeonWindow);
102						if (CanGenerateFeatureAt(data, dungeonWindow, center.X, center.Y))
103						{
104							dungeonWindow.GenerateFeature(data, center.X, center.Y);
105						}
106					}
107					else
108					{
109						num = Math.Min(7, Math.Max(3, InnerBounds.Width / 5));
110						if (num % 2 == 0)
111						{
112							num++;
113						}
114						dungeonWindowBasicSettings.Width = Math.Max(3, num);
115						dungeonWindowBasicSettings.Height = Math.Max(5, InnerBounds.Height / 3);
116						DungeonWindow dungeonWindow = new DungeonWindowBasic(dungeonWindowBasicSettings);
117						center = GetRoomCenterForDungeonFeature(data, dungeonWindow);
118						if (CanGenerateFeatureAt(data, dungeonWindow, center.X, center.Y))
119						{
120							dungeonWindow.GenerateFeature(data, center.X, center.Y);
121						}
122						dungeonWindowBasicSettings.Height -= 2;
123						dungeonWindow = new DungeonWindowBasic(dungeonWindowBasicSettings);
124						if (CanGenerateFeatureAt(data, dungeonWindow, center.X - num - 2, center.Y))
125						{
126							dungeonWindow.GenerateFeature(data, center.X - num - 2, center.Y);
127						}
128						dungeonWindow = new DungeonWindowBasic(dungeonWindowBasicSettings);
129						if (CanGenerateFeatureAt(data, dungeonWindow, center.X + num + 2, center.Y))
130						{
131							dungeonWindow.GenerateFeature(data, center.X + num + 2, center.Y);
132						}
133					}
134				}
135			}
136			int liquidType = settings.StyleData.LiquidType;
137			if (liquidType >= 0)
138			{
139				FloodRoom((byte)liquidType);
140			}
141		}

298				{

303						tile.liquidType(liquidType);

378					}

380					{

387						}
388					}

390				}
391			}
392		}

	public static void CalculatePlatformsAndDoorsOnEdgesOfRoom(DungeonData dungeonData, DungeonBounds innerBounds, DungeonGenerationStyleData styleData, int? doorFluff = null, int? platformFluff = null)
	{
		UnifiedRandom genRand = WorldGen.genRand;
		if (styleData == null)
		{
			if (!WorldGen.InWorld(innerBounds.Center.X, innerBounds.Center.Y, 5))
			{
				return;
			}
			Tile tile = Main.tile[innerBounds.Center.X, innerBounds.Center.Y];
			styleData = DungeonGenerationStyles.GetStyleForWall(dungeonData.genVars.dungeonGenerationStyles, tile.wall);
			if (styleData == null && tile.active())
			{
				styleData = DungeonGenerationStyles.GetStyleForTile(dungeonData.genVars.dungeonGenerationStyles, tile.type);
			}
			if (styleData == null)
			{
				styleData = dungeonData.genVars.dungeonStyle;
			}
		}
		bool flag = styleData.Style == 0;
		ushort brickTileType = styleData.BrickTileType;
		ushort brickWallType = styleData.BrickWallType;
		int num = Math.Max(5, innerBounds.Left);
		int num2 = Math.Min(Main.maxTilesX - 5, innerBounds.Right);
		int num3 = Math.Max(5, innerBounds.Top);
		int num4 = Math.Min(Main.maxTilesY - 5, innerBounds.Bottom);
		bool flag2 = false;
		bool flag3 = false;
		for (int i = num; i <= num2; i++)
		{
			if (!flag2 && !Main.tile[i, num3 - 1].active())
			{
				DungeonPlatformData item = new DungeonPlatformData
				{
					Position = new Point(i, num3 - 1),
					InAHallway = false,
					OverrideStyle = styleData.GetPlatformStyle(genRand)
				};
				if (platformFluff.HasValue)
				{
					item.OverrideHeightFluff = platformFluff.Value;
				}
				dungeonData.dungeonPlatformData.Add(item);
				flag2 = true;
			}
			if (!flag3 && !Main.tile[i, num4 + 1].active())
			{
				DungeonPlatformData item2 = new DungeonPlatformData
				{
					Position = new Point(i, num4 + 1),
					InAHallway = false,
					OverrideStyle = styleData.GetPlatformStyle(genRand)
				};
				if (platformFluff.HasValue)
				{
					item2.OverrideHeightFluff = platformFluff.Value;
				}
				dungeonData.dungeonPlatformData.Add(item2);
				flag3 = true;
			}
			if (flag2 && flag3)
			{
				break;
			}
		}
		if (styleData.DoorItemTypes == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		int num5 = ((flag || styleData.DoorItemTypes.Length == 0) ? (-1) : styleData.DoorItemTypes[genRand.Next(styleData.DoorItemTypes.Length)]);
		bool flag4 = false;
		bool flag5 = false;
		for (int j = num3; j <= num4; j++)
		{
			if (!flag4 && !Main.tile[num - 1, j].active())
			{
				bool flag6 = doorFluff.HasValue && doorFluff.Value == 0;
				DungeonDoorData item3 = new DungeonDoorData
				{
					OverrideBrickTileType = brickTileType,
					OverrideBrickWallType = brickWallType,
					Position = new Point(num - 1, j),
					Direction = -1,
					InAHallway = false,
					SkipOtherDoorsCheck = flag6,
					SkipSpaceCheck = flag6,
					AlwaysClearArea = true
				};
				if (doorFluff.HasValue)
				{
					item3.OverrideWidthFluff = doorFluff.Value;
				}
				if (num5 >= 0)
				{
					PlacementDetails placementDetails = ItemID.Sets.DerivedPlacementDetails[num5];
					item3.OverrideStyle = placementDetails.tileStyle;
				}
				dungeonData.dungeonDoorData.Add(item3);
				flag4 = true;
			}
			if (!flag5 && !Main.tile[num2 + 1, j].active())
			{
				bool flag7 = doorFluff.HasValue && doorFluff.Value == 0;
				DungeonDoorData item4 = new DungeonDoorData
				{
					OverrideBrickTileType = brickTileType,
					OverrideBrickWallType = brickWallType,
					Position = new Point(num2 + 1, j),
					Direction = 1,
					InAHallway = false,
					SkipOtherDoorsCheck = flag7,
					SkipSpaceCheck = flag7,
					AlwaysClearArea = true
				};
				if (doorFluff.HasValue)
				{
					item4.OverrideWidthFluff = doorFluff.Value;
				}
				if (num5 >= 0)
				{
					PlacementDetails placementDetails2 = ItemID.Sets.DerivedPlacementDetails[num5];
					item4.OverrideStyle = placementDetails2.tileStyle;
				}
				dungeonData.dungeonDoorData.Add(item4);
				flag5 = true;
			}
			if (flag4 && flag5)
			{
				break;
			}
		}
	}

	public static void ChangeTileType(Tile tile, ushort tileType, bool resetTile, int paint = -1)
	{
		if (resetTile)
		{
			tile.ClearEverything();
		}
		tile.active(active: true);
		tile.Clear(TileDataType.Slope);
		tile.type = tileType;
		if (paint > -1)
		{
			tile.color((byte)paint);
		}
	}

	public static void ChangeWallType(Tile tile, ushort wallType, bool resetTile, int paint = -1)
	{
		if (resetTile)
		{
			tile.ClearEverything();
		}
		tile.wall = wallType;
		if (paint > -1)
		{
			tile.wallColor((byte)paint);
		}
	}

	public static int GetDualDungeonBrickSupportCutoffY(DungeonData data)
	{
		if (SpecialSeedFeatures.DungeonEntranceIsUnderground)
		{
			return data.genVars.outerPotentialDungeonBounds.Top - 5;
		}
		return data.genVars.outerPotentialDungeonBounds.Top - 10;
	}

	public static void UpdateDungeonProgress(GenerationProgress progress, float percentile, string debugString, bool noFormatting = false)
	{
		Main.statusText = debugString;
		if (progress != null)
		{
			if (noFormatting)
			{
				progress.MessageNoFormatting = debugString;
			}
			else
			{
				progress.Message = debugString;
			}
			progress.Set(percentile);
		}
	}

	public static Point SetOldManSpawnAndSpawnOldManIfDefaultDungeon(int x, int y, bool generating = false)
	{
		Point result = new Point(x, y);
		if (GenVars.CurrentDungeon == 0)
		{
			Main.dungeonX = result.X;
			Main.dungeonY = result.Y;
			if (generating)
			{
				int num = NPC.NewNPC(new EntitySource_WorldGen(), Main.dungeonX * 16 + 8, Main.dungeonY * 16, 37);
				Main.npc[num].homeless = false;
				Main.npc[num].homeTileX = Main.dungeonX;
				Main.npc[num].homeTileY = Main.dungeonY;
				if (Main.onlyShimmerOceanWorldsGeneration)
				{
					Main.npc[num].GivenName = "Old Man James";
				}
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	public static bool IsPointOfProtectionType(int i2, int j2, List<DungeonRoom> roomsInArea, ProtectionType protectionToCheck)
	{
		ProtectionType highestProtectionTypeFromPoint = GetHighestProtectionTypeFromPoint(i2, j2, roomsInArea);
		switch (protectionToCheck)
		{
		default:
			return highestProtectionTypeFromPoint == protectionToCheck;
		case ProtectionType.Tiles:
		case ProtectionType.Walls:
			if (highestProtectionTypeFromPoint != protectionToCheck)
			{
				return highestProtectionTypeFromPoint == ProtectionType.TilesAndWalls;
			}
			return true;
		case ProtectionType.TilesAndWalls:
			if (highestProtectionTypeFromPoint != protectionToCheck && highestProtectionTypeFromPoint != ProtectionType.Tiles)
			{
				return highestProtectionTypeFromPoint == ProtectionType.Walls;
			}
			return true;
		}
	}

	public static ProtectionType GetHighestProtectionTypeFromPoint(int i2, int j2, List<DungeonRoom> roomsInArea)
	{
		ProtectionType protectionType = ProtectionType.None;
		for (int k = 0; k < roomsInArea.Count; k++)
		{
			switch (roomsInArea[k].GetProtectionTypeFromPoint(i2, j2))
			{
			case ProtectionType.Tiles:
				protectionType = ((protectionType != ProtectionType.Walls) ? ProtectionType.Tiles : ProtectionType.TilesAndWalls);
				break;
			case ProtectionType.Walls:
				protectionType = ((protectionType != ProtectionType.Tiles) ? ProtectionType.Walls : ProtectionType.TilesAndWalls);
				break;
			case ProtectionType.TilesAndWalls:
				protectionType = ProtectionType.TilesAndWalls;
				break;
			}
			if (protectionType == ProtectionType.TilesAndWalls)
			{
				break;
			}
		}
		return protectionType;
	}

	public static DungeonRoom GetClosestRoomTo(List<DungeonRoom> roomsToCheck, Point point, DungeonRoomSearchSettings settings)
	{
		return GetClosestRoomTo(roomsToCheck, point.X, point.Y, settings);
	}

	public static DungeonRoom GetClosestRoomTo(List<DungeonRoom> roomsToCheck, int x, int y, DungeonRoomSearchSettings settings)
	{
		Vector2 value = new Vector2(x, y);
		DungeonRoom result = null;
		float num = 999999f;
		for (int i = 0; i < roomsToCheck.Count; i++)
		{
			DungeonRoom dungeonRoom = roomsToCheck[i];
			if (RoomCanBeChosen(dungeonRoom, settings))
			{
				if (dungeonRoom.OuterBounds.ContainsWithFluff(x, y, settings.Fluff))
				{
					return dungeonRoom;
				}
				float num2 = Vector2.Distance(value, dungeonRoom.Center.ToVector2());
				if (num2 < num)
				{
					result = dungeonRoom;
					num = num2;
				}
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	public static List<DungeonRoom> GetAllRoomsNearSpot(List<DungeonRoom> roomsToCheck, int x, int y, DungeonRoomSearchSettings settings)
	{
		List<DungeonRoom> list = new List<DungeonRoom>();
		for (int i = 0; i < roomsToCheck.Count; i++)
		{
			DungeonRoom dungeonRoom = roomsToCheck[i];
			if (RoomCanBeChosen(dungeonRoom, settings) && dungeonRoom.OuterBounds.ContainsWithFluff(x, y, settings.Fluff))
			{
				list.Add(dungeonRoom);
			}
		}
		return list;
	}

	public static bool IsConsideredDungeonTile(int tileType, bool allDungeons = false)

		if (allDungeons)

		}
		else if (GenVars.CurrentDungeonGenVars.isDungeonTile[tileType])
		{

		}
		return false;
	}

	public static bool IsHigherOrEqualTieredDungeonTile(DungeonData data, int currentTileType, int newTileType)
	{
		double tierForDungeonTile = GetTierForDungeonTile(data.genVars, currentTileType);
		double tierForDungeonTile2 = GetTierForDungeonTile(data.genVars, newTileType);
		return tierForDungeonTile >= tierForDungeonTile2;
	}

	{

		double tierForDungeonWall2 = GetTierForDungeonWall(data.genVars, newWallType);
		return tierForDungeonWall >= tierForDungeonWall2;
	}

	public static double GetTierForDungeonTile(DungeonGenVars genVars, int tileType)
	{
		if (WorldGen.SecretSeed.dualDungeons.Enabled)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < genVars.dungeonGenerationStyles.Count; i++)
			{
				DungeonGenerationStyleData dungeonGenerationStyleData = genVars.dungeonGenerationStyles[i];
				if (dungeonGenerationStyleData.Style == 1 && DungeonGenerationStyles.Spider.TileIsInStyle(tileType))
				{
					return (double)i + 0.25;
				}
				if (dungeonGenerationStyleData.Style == 1 && DungeonGenerationStyles.LivingWood.TileIsInStyle(tileType))
				{
					return (double)i + 0.5;
				}
				if (dungeonGenerationStyleData.Style == 1 && DungeonGenerationStyles.Shimmer.TileIsInStyle(tileType))
				{
					return (double)i + 0.75;
				}
				if (dungeonGenerationStyleData.Style == 8 && DungeonGenerationStyles.LivingMahogany.TileIsInStyle(tileType))
				{
					return (double)i + 0.33;
				}
				if (dungeonGenerationStyleData.Style == 8 && DungeonGenerationStyles.Beehive.TileIsInStyle(tileType))
				{
					return (double)i + 0.66;
				}
				if (dungeonGenerationStyleData.Style == 6 && DungeonGenerationStyles.Crystal.TileIsInStyle(tileType))
				{
					return (double)i + 0.5;
				}
				if (dungeonGenerationStyleData.Style == 0 && Main.tileDungeon[tileType])
				{
					return i;
				}
				if (dungeonGenerationStyleData.TileIsInStyle(tileType))
				{
					return i;
				}
			}
			if (Main.tileDungeon[tileType])
			{
				return -0.5;
			}
			return -1.0;
		}
		return (tileType > 0 && Main.tileDungeon[tileType]) ? 1f : (-1f);
	}

	public static double GetTierForDungeonWall(DungeonGenVars genVars, int wallType)
	{
		if (WorldGen.SecretSeed.dualDungeons.Enabled)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < genVars.dungeonGenerationStyles.Count; i++)
			{
				DungeonGenerationStyleData dungeonGenerationStyleData = genVars.dungeonGenerationStyles[i];
				if (dungeonGenerationStyleData.Style == 1 && DungeonGenerationStyles.Spider.WallIsInStyle(wallType))
				{
					return (double)i + 0.25;
				}
				if (dungeonGenerationStyleData.Style == 1 && DungeonGenerationStyles.LivingWood.WallIsInStyle(wallType))
				{
					return (double)i + 0.5;
				}
				if (dungeonGenerationStyleData.Style == 1 && DungeonGenerationStyles.Shimmer.WallIsInStyle(wallType))
				{
					return (double)i + 0.75;
				}
				if (dungeonGenerationStyleData.Style == 8 && DungeonGenerationStyles.LivingMahogany.WallIsInStyle(wallType))
				{
					return (double)i + 0.33;
				}
				if (dungeonGenerationStyleData.Style == 8 && DungeonGenerationStyles.Beehive.WallIsInStyle(wallType))
				{
					return (double)i + 0.66;
				}
				if (dungeonGenerationStyleData.Style == 6 && DungeonGenerationStyles.Crystal.WallIsInStyle(wallType))
				{
					return (double)i + 0.5;
				}
				if (dungeonGenerationStyleData.Style == 0 && Main.wallDungeon[wallType])
				{
					return i;
				}
				if (dungeonGenerationStyleData.WallIsInStyle(wallType))
				{
					return i;
				}
			}
			if (Main.wallDungeon[wallType])
			{
				return -0.5;
			}
			return -1.0;
		}
		return (wallType > 0 && Main.wallDungeon[wallType]) ? 1f : (-1f);
	}

	public static void CreatePotentialDungeonBounds(out DungeonBounds innerBounds, out DungeonBounds outerBounds, bool leftDungeon, double percentInMiddle = 0.02, double percentOnEdges = 0.02, double percentOnTop = -1.0, double percentOnBottom = -1.0, int innerBuffer = 10)
	{
		if (percentOnTop == -1.0)
		{
			percentOnTop = ((!SpecialSeedFeatures.DungeonEntranceIsUnderground) ? ((Main.worldSurface + 10.0) / (double)Main.maxTilesY) : ((GenVars.worldSurfaceHigh + 10.0) / (double)Main.maxTilesY));
		}
		if (percentOnBottom == -1.0)
		{
			percentOnBottom = ((double)Main.UnderworldLayer - 10.0) / (double)Main.maxTilesY;
		}
		double num = percentInMiddle / 2.0;
		_ = (double)Main.maxTilesX / 4200.0;
		int num2 = (leftDungeon ? ((int)((double)Main.maxTilesX * percentOnEdges)) : ((int)((double)Main.maxTilesX * (0.5 + num))));
		int num3 = (leftDungeon ? ((int)((double)Main.maxTilesX * (0.5 - num))) : (Main.maxTilesX - (int)((double)Main.maxTilesX * percentOnEdges)));
		int num4 = (int)((double)Main.maxTilesY * percentOnTop);
		int num5 = (int)((double)Main.maxTilesY * percentOnBottom);
		outerBounds = new DungeonBounds();
		outerBounds.SetBounds(num2, num4, num3, num5);
		innerBounds = new DungeonBounds();
		innerBounds.SetBounds(num2 + innerBuffer, num4 + innerBuffer, num3 - innerBuffer, num5 - innerBuffer);
	}

	public static bool InAnyPotentialDungeonBounds(int x, int y, int fluff = 0, bool inner = false)
	{
		int iteration;
		return InAnyPotentialDungeonBounds(out iteration, x, y, fluff, inner);
	}

	public static bool InAnyPotentialDungeonBounds(out int iteration, int x, int y, int fluff = 0, bool inner = false)
	{
		iteration = -1;
		for (int i = 0; i < GenVars.dungeonGenVars.Count; i++)
		{
			DungeonGenVars dungeonGenVars = GenVars.dungeonGenVars[i];
			if ((inner && dungeonGenVars.innerPotentialDungeonBounds.ContainsWithFluff(x, y, fluff)) || (!inner && dungeonGenVars.outerPotentialDungeonBounds.ContainsWithFluff(x, y, fluff)))
			{
				iteration = i;
				return true;
			}
		}
		return false;
	}

	public static bool IntersectsAnyPotentialDungeonBounds(Rectangle rect, bool inner = false)
	{
		int iteration;
		return IntersectsAnyPotentialDungeonBounds(out iteration, rect, inner);
	}

	public static bool IntersectsAnyPotentialDungeonBounds(out int iteration, Rectangle rect, bool inner = false)
	{
		iteration = -1;
		for (int i = 0; i < GenVars.dungeonGenVars.Count; i++)
		{
			DungeonGenVars dungeonGenVars = GenVars.dungeonGenVars[i];
			if ((inner && dungeonGenVars.innerPotentialDungeonBounds.Intersects(rect)) || (!inner && dungeonGenVars.outerPotentialDungeonBounds.Intersects(rect)))
			{
				iteration = i;
				return true;
			}
		}
		return false;
	}

	public static Rectangle GetExpandedDungeonAreaFromPoint(int x, int y)
	{
		int num = x;
		int num2 = x;
		int num3 = y;
		int num4 = y;
		for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++)
		{
			num = x;
			num2 = x;
			while (!Main.tile[num, y].active() && IsConsideredDungeonWall(Main.tile[num, y].wall))
			{
				num--;
			}
			num++;
			for (; !Main.tile[num2, y].active() && IsConsideredDungeonWall(Main.tile[num2, y].wall); num2++)
			{
			}
			num2--;
			x = (num + num2) / 2;
			num3 = y;
			num4 = y;
			while (!Main.tile[x, num3].active() && IsConsideredDungeonWall(Main.tile[x, num3].wall))
			{
				num3--;
			}
			num3++;
			for (; !Main.tile[x, num4].active() && IsConsideredDungeonWall(Main.tile[x, num4].wall); num4++)
			{
			}
			num4--;
			y = (num3 + num4) / 2;
		}
		num = x;
		num2 = x;
		while (!Main.tile[num, y].active() && !Main.tile[num, y - 1].active() && !Main.tile[num, y + 1].active())
		{
			num--;
		}
		num++;
		for (; !Main.tile[num2, y].active() && !Main.tile[num2, y - 1].active() && !Main.tile[num2, y + 1].active(); num2++)
		{
		}
		num2--;
		num3 = y;
		num4 = y;
		while (!Main.tile[x, num3].active() && !Main.tile[x - 1, num3].active() && !Main.tile[x + 1, num3].active())
		{
			num3--;
		}
		num3++;
		for (; !Main.tile[x, num4].active() && !Main.tile[x - 1, num4].active() && !Main.tile[x + 1, num4].active(); num4++)
		{
		}
		num4--;
		return new Rectangle(num, num3, num4 - num3, num2 - num);
	}

	public static int CalculateFloodedTileCountFromShapeData(DungeonBounds innerBounds, ShapeData data)
	{
		Point16[] array = data.GetData().ToArray();
		int num = 0;
		for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
		{
			if (array[i].Y > innerBounds.Center.Y)
			{
				num++;
			}
		}
		return num;
	}
}

107		}
108	
109		public bool CanGenerateFeatureInArea(IDungeonFeature feature, Rectangle hitbox)
110		{
111			for (int i = hitbox.Left; i <= hitbox.Right; i++)
112			{
113				for (int j = hitbox.Top; j <= hitbox.Bottom; j++)
114				{
115					if (!CanGenerateFeatureAt(feature, i, j))
116					{
117						return false;
118					}
119				}
120			}
121			return true;
122		}
123	
124		public bool CanGenerateFeatureAt(IDungeonFeature feature, int x, int y)
125		{
126			if (!WorldGen.InWorld(x, y, 5))
127			{
128				return false;
129			}
130			if (Main.tile[x, y].wall == 350)
131			{
132				return false;
133			}
134			if (dungeonEntrance.Bounds.Contains(x, y) && !dungeonEntrance.CanGenerateFeatureAt(this, feature, x, y))
135			{
136				return false;
137			}
138			for (int i = 0; i < protectedDungeonBounds.Count; i++)
139			{
140				if (protectedDungeonBounds[i].Contains(x, y))
141				{
142					return false;
143				}
144			}
145			for (int j = 0; j < dungeonFeatures.Count; j++)
146			{
147				IDungeonFeature dungeonFeature = dungeonFeatures[j];
148				if (dungeonFeature is DungeonFeature)
149				{
150					DungeonFeature dungeonFeature2 = (DungeonFeature)dungeonFeature;
151					if (dungeonFeature2.generated && dungeonFeature2.Bounds.Contains(x, y) && !dungeonFeature2.CanGenerateFeatureAt(this, feature, x, y))
152					{
153						return false;
154					}
155				}
156			}
157			for (int k = 0; k < dungeonRooms.Count; k++)
158			{
159				DungeonRoom dungeonRoom = dungeonRooms[k];
160				if (dungeonRoom.generated && dungeonRoom.OuterBounds.Contains(x, y) && !dungeonRoom.CanGenerateFeatureAt(this, feature, x, y))
161				{
162					return false;
163				}
164			}
165			return true;
166		}
167	
168		public bool IsAnyRoomInSpot(out DungeonRoom roomFound, int x, int y, DungeonRoomSearchSettings settings)
169		{
170			roomFound = null;
171			for (int i = 0; i < dungeonRooms.Count; i++)
172			{
173				DungeonRoom dungeonRoom = dungeonRooms[i];
174				if (DungeonUtils.RoomCanBeChosen(dungeonRoom, settings) && dungeonRoom.InnerBounds.ContainsWithFluff(x, y, settings.Fluff))
175				{
176					roomFound = dungeonRoom;
177					return true;
178				}
179			}
180			return false;
181		}
182	}
183	

```

119	class DBnd {
120	  L = 10; R = 10; T = 10; B = 10;
121	  has = false;
122	  constructor(public w: number, public h: number) {}
123	  private cx(v: number): number { return Math.max(10, Math.min(this.w - 10, v)); }
124	  private cy(v: number): number { return Math.max(10, Math.min(this.h - 10, v)); }
125	  set(l: number, t: number, r: number, b: number): void {
126	    this.L = this.cx(l); this.R = this.cx(r); this.T = this.cy(t); this.B = this.cy(b);
127	    this.calc();
128	  }
129	  calc(): void {
130	    if (this.R <= this.L) this.R = this.L + 1;
131	    if (this.B <= this.T) this.B = this.T + 1;
132	    this.has = true;
133	  }
134	  upd(x: number, y: number): void;
135	  upd(minX: number, minY: number, maxX: number, maxY: number): void;
136	  upd(a: number, b: number, c?: number, d?: number): void {
137	    if (c === undefined || d === undefined) {
138	      if (a < this.L) this.L = this.cx(a);
139	      if (a > this.R) this.R = this.cx(a);
140	      if (b < this.T) this.T = this.cy(b);
141	      if (b > this.B) this.B = this.cy(b);
142	    } else {
143	      if (a < this.L) this.L = this.cx(a);
144	      if (c > this.R) this.R = this.cx(c);
145	      if (b < this.T) this.T = this.cy(b);
146	      if (d > this.B) this.B = this.cy(d);
147	    }
148	    this.has = true;
149	  }
150	  topSet(t: number): void { this.T = this.cy(t); this.has = true; }
151	  inflate(a: number): void { this.set(this.L - a, this.T - a, this.R + a, this.B + a); }
152	  contains(x: number, y: number): boolean {
153	    return this.has && x >= this.L && x < this.R && y >= this.T && y < this.B;
154	  }
155	  containsF(x: number, y: number, fluff: number): boolean {
156	    if (fluff === 0) return this.contains(x, y);
157	    return this.has && x >= this.L - fluff && x < this.R + fluff && y >= this.T - fluff && y < this.B + fluff;
158	  }
159	}

185	  flipGenSolid([481, 482, 483], false);

189	  // 掷骰适配：ri(a,b)=Next(a,b+1)；rn(n)=Next(n)；rd()=NextDouble()；raw()=Next()
190	  const ri = (a: number, b: number): number => rng.int(a, b);
191	  const rn = (n: number): number => rng.nextInt(n);
192	  const rd = (): number => rng.next();
193	  const raw = (): number => rng.vanilla.next();

195	  const brick = theme === 0 ? BLUE : theme === 1 ? GREEN : PINK;

200	  const potTp = (ws + 10.0) / h, potBt = ((h - 200) - 10.0) / h;

202	  const potR = gs.dungeonSide > 0 ? w - Math.trunc(w * 0.05) : Math.trunc(w * 0.45);
203	  const potT = Math.max(10, Math.min(h - 10, Math.trunc(h * potTp)));

205	  const potContains = (x: number, y: number): boolean =>
206	    x >= Math.max(10, Math.min(w - 10, potL)) && x < Math.max(10, Math.min(w - 10, potR)) && y >= potT && y < potB;

243	  {

245	    let solid = false;
246	    for (let k = 0; k < 10; k++) if (dgSolid(x0, y0 + k)) { solid = true; break; }
247	    if (!solid) { while (y0 < cap && !dgSolid(x0, y0 + 10)) y0++; }
248	    if (solid) {

279	  function prot(x: number, y: number, list: DRoom[]): number {
280	    let p = 0;
281	    for (const rm of list) {
282	      const q: number = rm.outer.contains(x, y) ? 2 : 0;
283	      if (q === 1) p = p === 2 ? 3 : 1;
284	      else if (q === 2) p = p === 1 ? 3 : 2;
285	      if (p === 3) break;
286	    }
287	    return p;
288	  }

// DungeonHall.CanPlaceTileAt（DungeonHall.cs:161）——普通世界：仅非地牢墙处置砖
bool DgCanPlaceTileAt(int idx)
{
    if (DgIsDW(wal[idx]))
    {
        if (act[idx] != 0)
        {
            if (DgIsDT(typ[idx])) return false;          // IsHigherOrEqualTieredDungeonTile：tileDungeon 同档
            return typ[idx] != dgCrack;
        }
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}
// LegacyDungeonLayoutProvider.LegacyDungeonLayout（:25-98）
void DgLayout(int steps)
{
    int maxSteps = steps;
    dgBounds = new DBnd();
    dgBounds.Set(dgPosX, dgPosY, dgPosX, dgPosY);
    // ★原版 LegacyDungeonLayoutProvider.cs:36-43：起始房前先
    //   `hallSettings.RandomSeed = Next()` + `roomSettings.RandomSeed = Next()` 各掷一颗
    //   （两 settings 仅作模板、每次生成前都会重设种子，故这两掷是纯流消耗）。
    //   曾镜像 JS 旧偏差删掉这两掷（起始房种子前移 2 → 全地牢分叉）——JS 已补回，翻回原版。
    int hallSeed = (int)r.Next();      // legacyDungeonHallSettings.RandomSeed（:37）
    int roomSeed = (int)r.Next();      // legacyDungeonRoomSettings.RandomSeed（:42）
    DgRoom(new DRoom { Seed = (int)r.Next(), X = dgPosX, Y = dgPosY, Start = true });   // 起始房（:44-50）
    int roomDelay = 5;
    while (steps > 0)
    {
        dgBounds.Upd(dgPosX, dgPosY);
        steps--;
        if (roomDelay > 0) roomDelay--;
        if ((roomDelay == 0) & ((int)r.Next(3) == 0))   /* 1456 LegacyDungeonLayoutProvider.cs:61: 非短路and, roomDelay>0 照耗一骰 */
        {
            roomDelay = 5;
            if ((int)r.Next(2) == 0)
            {
                int sx = dgPosX, sy = dgPosY;
                DgHall(new DHall { Seed = (int)r.Next() });
                if ((int)r.Next(2) == 0) DgHall(new DHall { Seed = (int)r.Next() });
                DgRoom(new DRoom { Seed = (int)r.Next(), X = dgPosX, Y = dgPosY, Start = false });
                dgPosX = sx; dgPosY = sy;
            }
            else
            {
                DgRoom(new DRoom { Seed = (int)r.Next(), X = dgPosX, Y = dgPosY, Start = false });
            }
        }
        else
        {
            DgHall(new DHall { Seed = (int)r.Next() });
        }
    }
    DgRoom(new DRoom { Seed = (int)r.Next(), X = dgPosX, Y = dgPosY, Start = false });   // 尾房（:94-96）
}

The layout loops are identical between versions. So the true vanilla behavior: **rooms do not advance the position**.

The JS `dgRoom` sets `posX = Math.trunc(px); posY = Math.trunc(py);` at the end, and the oracle does the same (`dgPosX = (int)px; dgPosY = (int)py;`). Both are wrong vs vanilla!

using System.Collections.Generic;
using Terraria.GameContent.Generation.Dungeon.Features;
using Terraria.GameContent.Generation.Dungeon.Rooms;
using Terraria.WorldBuilding;

namespace Terraria.GameContent.Generation.Dungeon;

public static class DungeonGenerationStyles
{
	private class ShimmerStyleData : DungeonGenerationStyleData
	{
		public override bool CanGenerateFeatureAt(DungeonData data, DungeonRoom room, IDungeonFeature feature, int x, int y)
		{
			if (!(feature is DungeonPitTrap))
			{
				return !(feature is DungeonWindow);
			}
			return false;
		}
	}

	private class LivingWoodStyleData : DungeonGenerationStyleData
	{
		public override bool CanGenerateFeatureAt(DungeonData data, DungeonRoom room, IDungeonFeature feature, int x, int y)
		{
			if (!(feature is DungeonGlobalSpikes))
			{
				return !(feature is DungeonPitTrap);
			}
			return false;
		}

		public override void GetBookshelfMinMaxSizes(int defaultMin, int defaultMax, out int min, out int max)
		{
			min = 3;
			max = 7;
		}
	}

	private class BeehiveStyleData : DungeonGenerationStyleData
	{
		public override bool CanGenerateFeatureAt(DungeonData data, DungeonRoom room, IDungeonFeature feature, int x, int y)
		{
			if (!(feature is DungeonGlobalPaintings) && !(feature is DungeonGlobalSpikes) && !(feature is DungeonPitTrap))
			{
				return !(feature is DungeonWindow);
			}
			return false;
		}
	}

	private class TempleStyleData : DungeonGenerationStyleData
	{
		public override bool CanGenerateFeatureAt(DungeonData data, DungeonRoom room, IDungeonFeature feature, int x, int y)
		{
			if (!(feature is DungeonPitTrap))
			{
				return !(feature is DungeonPillar);
			}
			return false;
		}
	}

	public static DungeonGenerationStyleData GetStyleForTile(List<DungeonGenerationStyleData> styles, int tileType)
	{
		foreach (DungeonGenerationStyleData style in styles)
		{
			if (style.TileIsInStyle(tileType))
			{
				return style;
			}
			if (style.SubStyles == null || style.SubStyles.Count <= 0)
			{
				continue;
			}
			foreach (DungeonGenerationStyleData subStyle in style.SubStyles)
			{
				if (subStyle.TileIsInStyle(tileType))
				{
					return subStyle;
				}
			}
		}
		return null;
	}

	public static DungeonGenerationStyleData GetStyleForWall(List<DungeonGenerationStyleData> styles, int wallType)
	{
		foreach (DungeonGenerationStyleData style in styles)
		{
			if (style.WallIsInStyle(wallType))
			{
				return style;
			}
			if (style.SubStyles == null || style.SubStyles.Count <= 0)
			{
				continue;
			}
			foreach (DungeonGenerationStyleData subStyle in style.SubStyles)
			{
				if (subStyle.WallIsInStyle(wallType))
				{
					return subStyle;
				}
			}
		}
		return null;
	}
}

// 地牢生成验证：完整管线生成后，地牢内部应存在 7/8/9 墙 + 地牢砖；
// world.dungeonX/Y 应指向实际结构（DungeonPass 重掷后的最终位置）
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { generateWorld } from '../src/world/gen/WorldGen';

describe('地牢生成（DungeonPass）', () => {
  it('完整管线后地牢墙存活 + dungeonX/Y 指向结构', async () => {
    const world = await generateWorld({ width: 4200, height: 1200, seedText: 'dungeon-probe-1' });
    const st = world.store;
    const dX = world.dungeonX, dY = world.dungeonY || Math.floor(world.groundLevel);
    // 扫地牢锚点周边 ±120 × (dY-20 .. rockLevel+200)
    let dungeonWalls = 0, dungeonBricks = 0;
    const y1 = Math.min(st.h - 10, Math.floor(world.rockLevel) + 200);
    for (let y = Math.max(10, dY - 20); y < y1; y++) {
      for (let x = Math.max(2, dX - 120); x < Math.min(st.w - 2, dX + 120); x++) {
        const i = st.idx(x, y);
        const wl = st.wall[i];
        if (wl === 7 || wl === 8 || wl === 9 || (wl >= 94 && wl <= 99)) dungeonWalls++;
        const d = st.type[i];
        // 地牢砖 = 内部 tile key v_41/v_43/v_44/481-483
        if (st.flags[i] && (d >= 0)) {
          const def = (st.type[i], undefined);
          void def;
        }
      }
    }
    // 砖统计换 key 反查
    const { TILE_BY_KEY } = await import('../src/data/tiles');
    const brickIds = ['v_41_blue_brick', 'v_43_green_brick', 'v_44_pink_brick'].map((k) => TILE_BY_KEY[k]);
    for (let y = Math.max(10, dY - 20); y < y1; y++) {
      for (let x = Math.max(2, dX - 120); x < Math.min(st.w - 2, dX + 120); x++) {
        const i = st.idx(x, y);
        if (st.flags[i] && brickIds.includes(st.type[i])) dungeonBricks++;
      }
    }
    console.log('dungeon:', { dX, dY, gl: world.groundLevel, dungeonWalls, dungeonBricks });
    expect(dungeonBricks).toBeGreaterThan(500);   // 地牢结构本体存在
    expect(dungeonWalls).toBeGreaterThan(200);    // 地牢背景墙存活
    expect(world.dungeonY).toBeGreaterThan(0);    // 入口 Y 已回填
  }, 120000);
});

	public static bool GenerateDungeonRegularChest(DungeonData data, DungeonGlobalBasicChests feature, DungeonGenerationStyleData styleData, DungeonBounds innerBounds)
	{
		int left = innerBounds.Left;
		int right = innerBounds.Right;
		int y = innerBounds.Center.Y;
		int bottom = innerBounds.Bottom;
		return GenerateDungeonRegularChest(data, feature, styleData, left, y, right, bottom);
	}

	public static bool GenerateDungeonRegularChest(DungeonData data, DungeonGlobalBasicChests feature, DungeonGenerationStyleData styleData, int minX, int minY, int maxX, int maxY)
	{
		UnifiedRandom genRand = WorldGen.genRand;
		int num = (int)Utils.Lerp(minX, maxX, genRand.NextDouble());
		int num2 = (int)Utils.Lerp(minY, maxY, genRand.NextDouble());
		if (!data.CanGenerateFeatureInArea(feature, num, num2, 1))
		{
			return false;
		}
		int itemType = -1;
		ushort chestTileType = 21;
		int chestStyle = 2;
		bool flag = false;
		switch (styleData.Style)
		{
		case 0:
			WorldGen.GetDungeonLootAndChestStyle(num, num2, ref itemType, ref chestStyle);
			flag = true;
			break;
		case 8:
		case 9:
		case 14:
			itemType = WorldGen.GetNextJungleChestItem();
			break;
		case 10:
			itemType = 1293;
			break;
		case 13:
			itemType = 832;
			if (genRand.Next(3) == 0)
			{
				itemType = 4281;
			}
			break;
		}
		if (!flag && styleData.ChestItemTypes.Length != 0)
		{
			PlacementDetails placementDetails = ItemID.Sets.DerivedPlacementDetails[styleData.ChestItemTypes[genRand.Next(styleData.ChestItemTypes.Length)]];
			chestTileType = (ushort)placementDetails.tileType;
			chestStyle = placementDetails.tileStyle;
		}
		if (itemType == 0 && genRand.Next(2) == 0)
		{
			return true;
		}
		bool num3 = WorldGen.AddBuriedChest(num, num2, itemType, notNearOtherChests: false, chestStyle, trySlope: false, chestTileType);
		if (num3 && styleData.Style == 0)
		{
			GenVars.CurrentDungeonGenVars.dungeonLootStyle++;
		}
		return num3;
	}

IsLockedDoor: `type==10 && frameY in [594,646] && frameX < 54`. frameY 594..646 = style rows 11-12ish (594/54 = 11). So the "locked" doors are specific frameY rows (the dungeon locked door variants). The JS canKill blocks ALL doors — but wait, during the Dungeon pass, the only doors placed so far are by this same feature loop with styles 13 or 16/17/18. Style 16: frameY = 54*(16%36) = 864 — above 646 → not locked. Style 13: frameY=702 → not locked. So C# IsLockedDoor is FALSE for all doors in this pass → C# would KILL adjacent doors; JS blocks all → keeps them.

Hmm — so the JS `if (t === DOOR) return false;` with comment "IsLockedDoor(style13)" is WRONG relative to C# — C# would kill those doors (they're not locked). But wait — would a door adjacent to another door even occur? The door scan has SkipOtherDoorsCheck (checks 20×20 for type 10) for most doors... unless another door was placed later than this scan. Doors are placed sequentially; an earlier door could be within ±2 columns of a later door's column only if the other-doors check failed to see it — the check scans ±20 columns so it would see it. So adjacent-door kills shouldn't trigger in practice. The deviation is effectively unreachable. Leave as is (document).

Also the chest protection in CanKillTile: it blocks killing a tile when the tile ABOVE it is a chest-family (21/26/72/77/88/467/488) with a different type — protecting chest supports. JS blocks killing CHEST tiles themselves — different semantic but in the door context: killing tiles beside the door column (±1/±2) — a chest could be adjacent (placed by BiomeChests/BasicChests earlier!). C# protects the tile BELOW a chest (support). JS protects the chest tile itself. Partial mismatch — but chests are 2×2 with tile above being chest → the ±1/±2 column neighbors of the door could include a chest's support tile. C# would refuse to kill the support under a chest; JS would kill it (JS only skips chest tiles themselves). Edge case. Given the door columns are filled with brick and the neighbor kills target existing active tiles — possible but rare. I'll note it in the report as a known micro-approximation, not fix (fixing requires the chest-family-above check; cheap to add actually... but it also has the tree-trunk check etc. The scope is potentially unbounded; the previous audits accepted this. I'll document).

Now — the report. Also I should double-check the two fixes that could interact with tests/golden checkpoints. I can't run caves-checkpoint (mainline busy + not in my task list). Fine.

Also — should gs.dungeonEntX be posX too? JS :2480: `gs.dungeonEntX = dungeonX; gs.dungeonEntY = dungeonY;` — dungeonX = trunc(cx) from the OLD MAN spawn (SetOldManSpawn at (int)zero.X, num6) — that's Main.dungeonX/dungeonY semantics ✓ separate field, correct.

Implementing fix 8 now.

ClearEverything 与 JS\"flags=0 保留 type\"**：C# 清 type 归 0，JS 留旧 type\n  （inactive 态）。哈希若按全字段比会差、按 active+wall+type of active tiles 比为零差。\n\n## 5. oracle 转录清单（tools/golden/caves-oracle.cs，主线占用中，待同步）\n\n行为修正（与 JS 本次修复一一对应）：\n1. `DgRoom` 尾部 `dgPosX=(int)px; dgPosY=(int)py;`（:4495）删除（A1）。\n2. `DgEntHall` `steps=Next(10,30)` 上移到 `num=Next(5,9)` 之后（:4776-4790）（A2）。\n3. 入口门洞 15×3 清空循环补 `(num2>0&&x<valX)||(num2<0&&x>valX)→wall=dgWall`\n   （valX=num2<0 时 cx-1 后）（A3）。\n4. `DDoor` 走廊门 Fluff=10（房间 3 不变）（B1）。\n5. 平台候选带高度 fluff：房间 3 / 走廊·入口 5（B2）。\n6. 书架平台 frameY=18*shelfStyles[墙变体档]（B3）。\n7. 吊灯资格门：wall==主砖墙（B4）。\n8. `dgPosX` 终值（爬升终点）作为金字塔/神庙段消费的 generatingDungeonPositionX（A4）。\n\n转录所需 GenState/参数字段（oracle 侧需等价承载）：\n- 读：`dungeonTheme`（Dunes 掷）、`dungeonEntSeed`（Dunes 掷）、`dungeonSide`、\n  `dungeonLocation`（Reset 掷）、`crimson`、`lavaLine`、`worldSurface`、`rockLevel`。\n- 写（本 pass 收尾回填）：`generatingDungeonPositionX`=爬升终点、`dungeonY`=老头锚 Y\n  （=Main.dungeonY，第二段 0.5 盒底）、`dungeonEntX/dungeonEntY`=入口门厅站立点。\n- 结构参数（1456 常量，Default 世界全部=1.0/0.5）：hallStrengthScalar=hallStepScalar=\n  roomStrengthScalar=roomStepScalar=globalFeatureScalar=dungeonStepScalar=1.0、\n  hallInteriorToExteriorRatio=roomInteriorToExteriorRatio=0.5、\n  hallSlantVariantScalar=roomSlantVariantScalar=1.0、CrackedBrickChance=0.166、\n  ZigzagChance=0.66（DungeonHallSettings.cs:14-21）。\n\n## 6. 验证\n\n- `npx tsc --noEmit`：DungeonPass/StructuresPass/GenState 零错（余错均为未触碰的\n  既有测试文件）。\n- `npx vitest run tests/dungeon-walls.test.ts`（砖 21088/墙 28548，结构完整）✓\n- `npx vitest run tests/chest-frames.test.ts` ✓\n- `npx vitest run tests/dungeon-spawn.test.ts`（守卫 68 池 + 常规池双用例）✓\n- 未跑 tests/caves-checkpoint.test.ts（主线占用；A1/A2/A3/B1/B4 会使 dungeonL 及\n  后续检查点哈希相对**当前** oracle 漂移——属预期，待 oracle 按 §5 同步后回归）。\n"

## 审计结论

**JS 侧找到 8 处真偏差并全部修复**（其中 7 处与 caves-oracle.cs 共享——oracle 转录清单已写入报告 §5）：

### 修复（game/src/world/gen/vanilla/DungeonPass.ts）
1. **【严重】房间不该推进 generatingDungeonPosition**（:406）：1456 全 Rooms 目录零赋值（仅 LegacyDungeonHall.cs:862 / LegacyEntranceDungeonHall.cs:367 两处写；1405 同构佐证）。曾 `posX=trunc(px)` 使起始房后首走廊、room-only 分支后续走廊全体错位——原版"同点叠房团"形态由此而来。
2. **【严重】入口走廊 steps 掷序**（:641-645）：C# 是 `Next(5,9)`→`Next(10,30)`→方向掷（:131→:136→:148），JS 曾把 steps 挪到方向掷后，整条爬升轨迹错位。
3. **入口门洞内半边墙回写缺失**（:979-994）：C# :606-611 清空时按 num2 方向对内半边写 brickWallType。
4. **走廊门扫描窗 fluff 0→10**（:1029）：C# num4 缺省 10（20 列窗），仅房间门覆写 3——JS 曾只扫门位单列，大量走廊门放不出。
5. **房间平台高度 fluff 5→3**（DPlat.hf，:172/:1451）：OverrideHeightFluff 语义。
6. **书架平台样式错用 platformStyle**（:1759）：应为 shelfStyles[墙变体三档]（SetupDungeonDataVariables 的 Next(9,13)×3）。
7. **吊灯资格门**（:1842）：仅主砖墙可放吊灯，变体墙直接落灯笼。
8. **gs.generatingDungeonPositionX 终值错**（:2513）：pass 序 Dungeon#36 先于 Pyramids#44/LihzahrdTemple#50，cs:15450/:15991 读到的是**爬升走廊终点**（每条 LegacyEntranceDungeonHall :367 覆写），:394 最高房中心是瞬态——曾回填 topX 使金字塔回避带平移数百格。同步修正 GenState.ts/StructuresPass.ts 注释。

### 已核实一致（防复审重复）
Layout 主循环（非短路 & 掷、模板双种子）、LegacyDungeonHall 全掷序+float32 fround+D2 else-if 链、pit 陷阱（protectedDungeonBounds 恒空，挡板是 PitTrap.Bounds+CanGenerateFeatureAt，JS 建模等价）、Doors/Spikes/WallVariants/Banners/Paintings/BasicChests 掷序、PlaceWall 门、Main.tileDungeon/wallDungeon 集合、Default 样式无门/平台样式掷。4 处可接受近似（prot 返 Walls、canKill 锁门/箱支撑、裂砖单色集、ClearEverything type 残留）留档未动。

- 报告：`game/docs/worldgen/dungeon-js-audit-2026-08-13.md`（调用图+掷骰清单、逐条差异+行号、oracle 转录 8 项清单 + GenState 读写字段表 + 1456 结构参数常量表）
